\chapter{Characterizing Behaviour-Driven Biopsychosocial Subtypes of
Pediatric
Concussion}\label{characterizing-behaviour-driven-biopsychosocial-subtypes-of-pediatric-concussion}

This book contains documented R code (formatted as .qmd files) used to
identify and characterize biopsychosocial subtypes of pediatric
concussion in the Adolescent Brain Cognitive Development study that are
driven by separation across behavioural measures.

To work through the code used, you will need:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  R (at least version 4.1.0)
\item
  The \texttt{abcdutils} package, available at
  \href{https://github.com/BRANCHlab/abcdutils}{BRANCHlab/abcdutils}
\item
  The \texttt{metasnf} package, available at
  \href{https://github.com/BRANCHlab/metasnf}{BRANCHlab/abcdutils}
\item
  Access to the following ABCD (5.0 release) data files:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    ph\_p\_otbi.csv
  \item
    abcd\_y\_lt.csv
  \item
    ph\_y\_pds.csv
  \item
    ph\_p\_pds.csv
  \item
    gish\_p\_gi.csv
  \item
    gish\_y\_gi.csv
  \item
    abcd\_p\_demo.csv
  \item
    ph\_p\_mhx.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_smr\_vol\_aseg.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_smr\_thk\_dst.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_smr\_thk\_dsk.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_smr\_area\_dst.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_smr\_area\_dsk.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_at.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_wm\_dsk.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_wm\_dst.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_rsfmr\_cor\_gp\_gp.csv
  \item
    mri\_y\_rsfmr\_cor\_gp\_aseg.csv
  \item
    mh\_y\_or.csv
  \item
    nt\_p\_stq.csv
  \item
    ph\_p\_saiq.csv
  \item
    ph\_y\_yrb.csv
  \item
    ce\_y\_psb.csv
  \item
    ce\_p\_psb.csv
  \item
    ce\_y\_fes.csv
  \item
    ce\_p\_fes.csv
  \item
    mh\_p\_asr.csv
  \item
    mh\_p\_cbcl.csv
  \item
    ph\_p\_sds.csv
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\part{Home}

\part{The ABCD dataset}

\chapter{Baseline Data Extraction}\label{baseline-data-extraction}

In this chapter, I identify subjects who have and who have not sustained
an mTBI prior to baseline data collection, and extract all features that
will be used for initial subtyping analyses.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(readr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(here)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(abcdutils)}

\section{Subjects}\label{subjects}

All information required for identifying children at a chronic stage
following mTBI as of baseline data collection is available in the
\texttt{abcd\_otbi01.txt} file, and in the \texttt{abcd\_lpohstbi01.txt}
file for longitudinal data. To identify subjects at a chronic stage
post-mTBI, two intermediate pieces of information must be calculated
from the original dataset:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  identifying which head injuries were mTBIs
\item
  identifying the time since the most recent mTBI
\end{itemize}

The structure of the Ohio State TBI screen as used for ABCD data
collection was not completely compatible with the most commonly used
definition of mTBI @WYA25C6X\#Blennow\_Etal\_2016, which requires at
least one of the following:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Any period of loss of consciousness
\item
  Any loss of memory
\item
  Any alteration in mental state
\item
  Focal neurological deficits
\end{itemize}

but none of the following:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  LOC over 30 minutes
\item
  A Glasgow Coma Scale score below 13
\item
  Post-traumatic amnesia (PTA) greater than 24 hours
\end{itemize}

A few aspects of the data collection process in the ABCD study precluded
the usage of this definition:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  No information was collected regarding the GCS
\item
  Feeling dazed and PTA were combined into a single variable, making it
  unclear which combination of symptoms was present
\item
  No information was collected regarding the duration of feeling dazed
  or experiencing PTA
\end{itemize}

Consequently, I defined mTBI as any injury that resulted in
\textbf{either}:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  LOC for less than 30 minutes (including 0 minutes) \textbf{AND} any
  duration of memory loss / feeling dazed
\item
  LOC for less than 30 minutes but greater than 0 minutes
\end{enumerate}

The fixed nature of the questionnaire also meant that some information
about multiple mTBIs sustained in the same way or about a very large
number of mTBIs throughout a child's life could have been lost (see the
previous section on identifying children with a history of mTBI). For my
analyses, I assume these edge cases are not common enough to
meaningfully change any obtained conclusions.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# mTBI subjects (latest mTBI at least 3 months prior to baseline collection}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_subjects }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_mtbi\_subjects}\NormalTok{(ph\_p\_otbi, abcd\_y\_lt, }\AttributeTok{min\_mpi =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Later analyses show some unusual data for subject: NDAR\_INVBU31791C.}
\CommentTok{\# They are removed from the uninjured subjects list.}

\CommentTok{\# Uninjured subjects (those with no injuries as of baseline collection)}
\NormalTok{uninjured\_subjects }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_uninjured\_subjects}\NormalTok{(ph\_p\_otbi, abcd\_y\_lt, }\AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_subjects, }\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mtbi\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{date =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(uninjured\_subjects, }\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"uninjured\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{date =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# Note that the longitudinal subjects have not been filtered in any way based on}
\CommentTok{\#  whether or not they also had an mTBI at baseline or another timepoint}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_subjects }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_csv}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_17\_mtbi\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{uninjured\_subjects }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_csv}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_17\_uninjured\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_subjects }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_subjects}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_17\_mtbi\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{uninjured\_subjects }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_subjects}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_17\_uninjured\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

A few types of injuries were handled differently from the rest in the
above code. For the \texttt{other\_loc} injury type, the questionnaire
asked for a total number of injuries with LOC as well as a total number
of injuries with LOC above 30 minutes. If their difference was above 0,
they were labelled as having had an mTBI. For the \texttt{other\_multi}
and \texttt{other\_other\_multi} injury types, insufficient information
was provided to determine the severity of the associated injuries.

For my analyses, I consider 3 months to be the threshold at which an
mTBI patient enters a chronic stage post-injury.

\section{Input variables}\label{input-variables}

\subsection{Acute symptoms}\label{acute-symptoms}

The acute symptom data is found within the TBI dataset. We are interesed
in the following acute symptoms:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  mechanism of injury
\item
  age
\item
  LOC information
\item
  Feeling dazed or having memory loss
\end{itemize}

A few more intermediate columns need to be added to isolate these
variables for the most recent mTBIs of the subjects.

\subsubsection{Feeling dazed}\label{feeling-dazed}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{as\_mtbi\_mem\_daze\_mtbi }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_mtbi\_mem\_daze}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    ph\_p\_otbi,}
\NormalTok{    abcd\_y\_lt,}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_subjects,}
    \AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    as\_mtbi\_mem\_daze\_mtbi,}
    \FunctionTok{input\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"as\_mem\_daze\_mtbi.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{date =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Mechanism of injury}\label{mechanism-of-injury}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{as\_mechanism\_mtbi }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_mtbi\_mechanism}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    ph\_p\_otbi,}
\NormalTok{    abcd\_y\_lt,}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_subjects,}
    \AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}

\subsection{Neuroimaging}\label{neuroimaging}

Variables of interest related to brain structure and function include:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Structural: T1-weighted multishell diffusion-weighted MRI data
\item
  Diffusion: Neurite density (restricted spectrum imaging)
\item
  Functional: resting-state functional MRI data
\end{itemize}

The 2019 publication by Hagler et al. @UD6THTQZ\#\#Hagler\_Etal\_2019
outlines the neuroimaging data present in the ABCD dataset in detail.

\subsubsection{Structural MRI}\label{structural-mri}

The sMRI variables of interest include subcortical volumes, and cortical
thicknesses, and cortical surface areas.

For cortical surface areas, the relevant file is abcd\_mrisdp10201,
which contains the cortical surface areas of \textasciitilde150 regions
(variables \texttt{mrisdp\_*} where * ranges from 1 to 151).

For cortical thicknesses, the relevant file is abcd\_mrisdp10201, which
contains the cortical thickness of \textasciitilde150 regions (variables
\texttt{mrisdp\_*} where * ranges from 303 to 453).

\paragraph{Subcortical volume}\label{subcortical-volume}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{sm\_subc\_v\_mtbi }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_subc\_v}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mri\_y\_smr\_vol\_aseg,}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_subjects,}
    \AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    sm\_subc\_v\_mtbi, }\FunctionTok{input\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"sm\_subc\_v\_mtbi.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{date =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{sm\_subc\_v\_uninj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_subc\_v}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mri\_y\_smr\_vol\_aseg,}
\NormalTok{    uninjured\_subjects,}
    \AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\paragraph{Cortical thickness}\label{cortical-thickness}

Both the Desikan and Destrieux cortical parcellations could have been
used for the cortical imaging data collected below. A comparison between
the two can be found in the appendix. Because there happens to be a few
more participants with calculated Desikan parcellations, that is what is
used here.

\subsubsection{Diffusion MRI}\label{diffusion-mri}

We are interested in the neurite density measures of white matter
regions in the brain. Previously, these measures were found in variables
containing the string ``rsindwm\_cdk'' within the file
\texttt{mri\_rsi\_p102.txt}. A change anounced in the ABCD 4.0 MRI
release notes indicates that neurite density is now referred to as
``restricted normalized directional'' and that the relevant white matter
variables contain the string ``rsirndwm'' within the file
\texttt{abcd\_drsip201.txt}.

See diffusion MRI chapter in appendix for full details.

\paragraph{White matter neurite
densities}\label{white-matter-neurite-densities}

Some potential summary neurite density meeasures:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  dmri\_rsirnd\_fib\_allfib (AtlasTrack fiber segmentation, all fibers)
\item
  dmri\_rsirndwm\_cdk\_mean (Desikan cortical parcellation peri-cortical
  white matter mean)
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# White matter neurite densities}
\NormalTok{dm\_all\_wmnd\_mtbi }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_all\_wmnd}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_at,}
\NormalTok{    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_wm\_dsk,}
    \AttributeTok{subjects =}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_subjects,}
    \AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    dm\_all\_wmnd\_mtbi, }\FunctionTok{input\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"dm\_all\_wmnd\_mtbi.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{date =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{dm\_all\_wmnd\_uninj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_all\_wmnd}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_at,}
\NormalTok{    mri\_y\_rsi\_rnd\_wm\_dsk,}
    \AttributeTok{subjects =}\NormalTok{ uninjured\_subjects,}
    \AttributeTok{t =} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    dm\_all\_wmnd\_uninj, }\FunctionTok{input\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"dm\_all\_wmnd\_uninj.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{date =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\chapter{Data Preprocessing}\label{data-preprocessing}

In this chapter, I

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Assign train and test sets of the data
\item
  Remove neuroimaging data for participants who do not meet ABCD's QC
\item
  Use mice imputation to impute missing data
\item
  Organize and export all data as nested lists
\end{enumerate}

\section{Importing Extracted Data}\label{importing-extracted-data}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(abcdutils)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(here)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(readr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(metasnf)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(mice)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(rlist)}

\NormalTok{input\_path }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{path\_maker}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{here}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/abcd/inputs"}\NormalTok{)))}
\NormalTok{raw\_path }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{path\_maker}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{here}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/abcd/raw"}\NormalTok{)))}
\NormalTok{core\_path }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{path\_maker}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{here}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/abcd/core"}\NormalTok{)))}
\NormalTok{misc\_path }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{path\_maker}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{here}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/abcd/misc"}\NormalTok{)))}

\section{Train/Test Split}\label{traintest-split}

To avoid having training and testing data inform each other during
imputation, we assign the splits prior to the imputation. This is only
done in our primary analysis group (baseline mTBI).

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{mtbi\_split }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{train\_test\_assign}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{train\_frac =} \FloatTok{0.8}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{subjects =}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_subjects}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_train }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_split}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"train"}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_test }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_split}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"test"}

\FunctionTok{write\_subjects}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train, }\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mtbi\_train\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\FunctionTok{write\_subjects}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_test, }\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mtbi\_test\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_train }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_subjects}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_18\_mtbi\_train\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_test }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_subjects}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sub\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_18\_mtbi\_test\_subjects.csv"}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{train\_filter }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df) \{}
\NormalTok{    filtered\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        df,}
\NormalTok{        df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"subjectkey"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_train}
\NormalTok{    )}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(filtered\_df)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{test\_filter }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df) \{}
\NormalTok{    filtered\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        df,}
\NormalTok{        df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"subjectkey"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_test}
\NormalTok{    )}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(filtered\_df)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_dfs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"subjects"} \OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{subjectkey =}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_subjects),}
    \StringTok{"as\_mem\_daze\_mtbi"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ as\_mem\_daze\_mtbi,}

    \StringTok{"mh\_headaches\_mtbi"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ mh\_headaches\_mtbi,}

    \StringTok{"sm\_subc\_v\_qc\_mtbi"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sm\_subc\_v\_qc\_mtbi,}

\section{Imputation}\label{imputation}

SNF requires complete data as input for integration.

Data will be imputed under the following schemes:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  5 imputations of mTBI data alone
\item
  5 imputations of uninjured data alone
\item
  5 imputations of mTBI + uninjured data together
\end{itemize}

Resulting in 10 versions of the mTBI data and 10 versions of the
uninjured data.

First, each group has their extracted dataframes merged into a single
one for imputation.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{mtbi\_train\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge\_df\_list}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train\_dfs, }\AttributeTok{join =} \StringTok{"full"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_test\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge\_df\_list}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_test\_dfs, }\AttributeTok{join =} \StringTok{"full"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{uninj\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge\_df\_list}\NormalTok{(uninj\_dfs, }\AttributeTok{join =} \StringTok{"full"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

A check of which columns have missing data.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{missing\_colnames\_mtbi }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colSums}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train\_df)) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{missing\_colnames\_mtbi}

\NormalTok{missing\_colnames\_uninj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colSums}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(uninj\_df)) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{missing\_colnames\_uninj}

\CommentTok{\# Remove the subjectkey variable}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_train\_df\_no\_subs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{char\_to\_fac}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train\_df[, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{])}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_test\_df\_no\_subs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{char\_to\_fac}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_test\_df[, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{])}
\NormalTok{uninj\_df\_no\_subs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{char\_to\_fac}\NormalTok{(uninj\_df[, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{])}

\CommentTok{\# Function that checks which variables have the biggest correlations}
\NormalTok{pairwise\_correlations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df, }\AttributeTok{threshold =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) \{}
    \CommentTok{\# Remove missing data to help calculate correlations}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(df)}
    \CommentTok{\# Remove non{-}numeric columns}
\NormalTok{    non\_numeric\_cols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sapply}\NormalTok{(df, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.numeric}\NormalTok{(x))}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df[, }\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{non\_numeric\_cols]}
    \CommentTok{\# Calculate correlations}
\NormalTok{    correlations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{cor}\NormalTok{(df)}
    \CommentTok{\# Ignore self{-}correlations}
    \FunctionTok{diag}\NormalTok{(correlations) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
    \CommentTok{\# Take absolute value of the correlations, as strong anti{-}correlations also}
    \CommentTok{\# lead to strong linear dependence}
\NormalTok{    abs\_cor }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(correlations)}
    \CommentTok{\# Create dataframe of all pairs of correlations}
\NormalTok{    pairs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{t}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{combn}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(abs\_cor), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{    paired\_cors }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(pairs, }\AttributeTok{cor =}\NormalTok{ abs\_cor[pairs])}
    \CommentTok{\# Filter to only correlations bigger than a specified threshold}
\NormalTok{    paired\_cors }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ paired\_cors[paired\_cors}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"cor"} \SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ threshold, ]}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(paired\_cors)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Function to help sort variables by pairwise correlations}
\NormalTok{top\_correlating\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df, }\AttributeTok{threshold =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) \{}
    \CommentTok{\# Dataframe highlighting strongest correlations}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{pairwise\_correlations}\NormalTok{(df, threshold)}
    \CommentTok{\# Iteratively build up an ordered correlations matrix by:}
    \CommentTok{\#   {-} Record one of the two variables contributing to the largest}
    \CommentTok{\#     pairwise correlation}
    \CommentTok{\#   {-} Remove that variables from the list of all pairs of correlations}
    \CommentTok{\#   {-} Repeat until all variables have been removed}
\NormalTok{    max\_cor\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{variable =} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{max\_cor =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{    i }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
    \ControlFlowTok{while}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{        i }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ i }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{        row\_with\_max\_cor }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"cor"} \SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"cor"}\NormalTok{))[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{        var\_with\_max\_cor }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df[row\_with\_max\_cor, }\StringTok{"X1"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{        max\_cor }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df[row\_with\_max\_cor, }\StringTok{"cor"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{        max\_cor\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{            max\_cor\_df,}
            \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
                \AttributeTok{variable =}\NormalTok{ var\_with\_max\_cor,}
                \AttributeTok{max\_cor =}\NormalTok{ max\_cor}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{        df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{            df,}
\NormalTok{            df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"X1"} \SpecialCharTok{!=}\NormalTok{ var\_with\_max\_cor }\SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"X2"} \SpecialCharTok{!=}\NormalTok{ var\_with\_max\_cor}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    \}}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(max\_cor\_df)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Prediction matrix construction}
\NormalTok{build\_pred\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df, }\AttributeTok{n\_predictors =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{    pred\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{matrix}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{nrow =} \FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df),}
        \AttributeTok{ncol =} \FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df),}
        \AttributeTok{data =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{    )}
    \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(pred\_mat) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(df)}
    \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(pred\_mat) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(df)}
    \FunctionTok{diag}\NormalTok{(pred\_mat) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{    pred\_mat\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{top\_correlating\_vars}\NormalTok{(df)}
\NormalTok{    vars\_to\_drop }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ pred\_mat\_vars}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"variable"}\NormalTok{[}\FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(n\_predictors)]}
    \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Dropping: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(vars\_to\_drop, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{)))}
\NormalTok{    pred\_mat[, vars\_to\_drop] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(pred\_mat)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#}
\CommentTok{\# Imputation {-} mTBI train}
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#}
\CommentTok{\# Doesn\textquotesingle{}t run (system is computationally singular)}
\NormalTok{imputed\_mtbi }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{futuremice}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_train\_df\_no\_subs,}
    \AttributeTok{m =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{maxit =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{parallelseed =} \DecValTok{42}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{build\_pred\_mat}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train\_df\_no\_subs, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Write and recover}
\FunctionTok{write.table}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat, }\FunctionTok{proc\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat.txt"}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.matrix}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{read.table}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{proc\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_18\_mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat.txt"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{storage.mode}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"double"}

\CommentTok{\# Runs}
\NormalTok{imputed\_mtbi\_train }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{futuremice}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_train\_df\_no\_subs,}
    \AttributeTok{m =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{n.core =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{maxit =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{parallelseed =} \DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{predictorMatrix =}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_train\_pred\_mat}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# mTBI solo imputed data}
\NormalTok{five\_imputed\_mtbi\_train }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{complete}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    imputed\_mtbi\_train,}
    \AttributeTok{action =} \StringTok{"long"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{include =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Write and recover}
\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    five\_imputed\_mtbi\_train,}
    \FunctionTok{input\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"five\_imputed\_mtbi\_train.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{five\_imputed\_mtbi\_train }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_csv}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{input\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2024\_04\_18\_five\_imputed\_mtbi\_train.csv"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#}
\CommentTok{\# Imputation {-} mTBI test}
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#}

\chapter{mTBI subsetting}\label{mtbi-subsetting}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{extract\_imputed }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(original\_df, imputed\_df, partition) \{}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(imputed\_df[imputed\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{".imp"} \SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ partition, }\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(original\_df)])}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{parse\_imputations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(original\_df\_list, complete\_imputed\_df, imps) \{}
\NormalTok{    parsed\_imputations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ original\_df\_list }\SpecialCharTok{|\textgreater{}}
        \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
            \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) \{}
\NormalTok{                imputations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
                \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(imps)) \{}
\NormalTok{                    imputations[[i]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{extract\_imputed}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{                        x,}
\NormalTok{                        complete\_imputed\_df,}
\NormalTok{                        i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{                    )}
\NormalTok{                \}}
                \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(imputations) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"imp\_"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(imps) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
                \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(imputations)}
\NormalTok{            \}}
\NormalTok{        )}
    \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(parsed\_imputations) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\_mtbi$"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(parsed\_imputations))}
    \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(parsed\_imputations) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\_uninj$"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(parsed\_imputations))}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(parsed\_imputations)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{pooled\_parsed\_imputations }\SpecialCharTok{|\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{()}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_train\_parsed\_imputations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{parse\_imputations}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_pooled\_parsed\_imputations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ pooled\_parsed\_imputations }\SpecialCharTok{|\textgreater{}}
    \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
        \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(variable) \{}
            \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{                variable,}
                \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(imputed\_df) \{}
\NormalTok{                    keep\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ imputed\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"subjectkey"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_train}
\NormalTok{                    filtered\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ imputed\_df[keep\_rows, ]}
                    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(filtered\_df)}
\NormalTok{                \}}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        \}}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{                variable,}

\NormalTok{                    keep\_rows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ imputed\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"subjectkey"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ uninj\_subjects}
\NormalTok{                    filtered\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ imputed\_df[keep\_rows, ]}
                    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(filtered\_df)}
\NormalTok{                \}}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        \}}
\NormalTok{    )}

\section{Constructing data\_lists}\label{constructing-data_lists}

We have:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  1 original unimputed mTBI dataset
\item
  1 original unimputed uninjured dataset
\item
  5 imputed mTBI datasets
\item
  5 imputed uninjured datasets
\item
  5 imputed mTBI + uninjured datasets
\end{itemize}

For the sake of brevity, these will be referred to with the following
notation:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  m = mTBI
\item
  u = uninjured
\item
  p = pooled
\item
  0 = original (unimputed)
\item
  1-5 = imputed dataset number
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{build\_dls }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(parsed\_imputations) \{}
\NormalTok{    data\_lists }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),}
        \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) \{}
            \FunctionTok{generate\_data\_list}\NormalTok{(}
                \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{                    parsed\_imputations}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"as\_mem\_daze"}\NormalTok{[[x }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]],}
                    \StringTok{"as\_mem\_daze"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"AS"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"discrete"}
\NormalTok{                ),}
                \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{                    parsed\_imputations}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"as\_mechanism"}\NormalTok{[[x }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]],}

                    \StringTok{"as\_loc"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"AS"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"discrete"}
\NormalTok{                ),}
                \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}

                    \StringTok{"D"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"discrete"}
\NormalTok{                ),}

                    \StringTok{"d\_mtbi\_age"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"D"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"discrete"}

\NormalTok{                ),}

                    \StringTok{"d\_race"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{                ),}

\chapter{MetaSNF}\label{metasnf}

meta clusters of the extracted data.

\section{Generating custom distance
measures}\label{generating-custom-distance-measures}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#\#}
\CommentTok{\# 2. Add standard normalized variations to the distance metrics list (also}

When picking out representative solutions, I'd like to preferentially go
for solutions that derived from solo rather than pooled imputations, as
those solutions have values for all features.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Function to try to lean on mTBI{-}including solutions when possible.}
\CommentTok{\# This function will pick from solo solutions unless over 70\% of the meta}
\CommentTok{\# cluster is pooled.}
\NormalTok{restrict\_fn }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x, }\AttributeTok{threshold =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{    x\_solo\_ind }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ x}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"imputation"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"m1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"m2"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"m3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"m4"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"m5"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    x\_pool\_ind }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ x}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"imputation"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"pm1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"pm2"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"pm3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"pm4"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"pm5"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    x\_solo }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ x[x\_solo\_ind, ]}
\NormalTok{    x\_pool }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ x[x\_pool\_ind, ]}
\NormalTok{    frac\_solo }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(x\_solo) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(x\_solo) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(x\_pool))}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (frac\_solo }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ threshold) \{}
        \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(x[x\_pool\_ind, ])}
\NormalTok{    \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
        \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(x[x\_solo\_ind, ])}
\NormalTok{    \}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_rep\_solutions }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_representative\_solutions}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_ari,}
    \AttributeTok{split\_vector =}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_split\_vec,}
    \AttributeTok{order =}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_ari\_order,}
    \AttributeTok{filter\_fn =}\NormalTok{ restrict\_fn,}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_esm}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Based on the heatmap, we\textquotesingle{}ll remove B, E, H, K (unstable meta clusters)}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_rep\_solutions }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_rep\_solutions,}
    \SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{label }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"B"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"E"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"H"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"K"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(mtbi\_rep\_solutions, }\FunctionTok{proc\_path}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mtbi\_rep\_solutions.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Key attributes of the cluster
solution}\label{key-attributes-of-the-cluster-solution}

Doesn't seem to be a big difference: - mtbi\_mem\_daze: 1 = 2 = 3 -
mtbi\_loc: 1 = 2 = 3 - mtbi\_age: 1 = 2 = 3 - previous\_mtbis: 1 = 2 = 3
- major\_wm\_ndi: 1 = 2 = 3 - pericortical\_wm\_ndi: 1 = 2 = 3 -
fmri\_cort\_cors: 1 = 2 = 3 - school\_programs: 1 = 2 = 3

Small difference: - mtbi\_mechanism 1 \textless{} 2 = 3 - age 1 = 2
\textless{} 3 - headache\_history: 3 \textless{} 1 = 2 -
fmri\_cort\_subcort\_cors: 1 \textless{} 3 \textless{} 2 -
cortical\_thickness: 1 \textless{} 2 = 3 - school\_programs: 1
\textless{} 2 \textless{} 3 - same\_sex\_friend: 3 \textgreater{} 1 = 2
- opp\_sex\_friend: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3

Seems to be a big difference: - sex (n\_female): 3
\textgreater\textgreater{} 1 \textgreater{} 2 - household\_income: 1
\textless\textless{} 2 = 3 - race (minority groups): 1
\textgreater\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - pubertal\_status: 2 \textless{} 1 = 3
- brain\_volume: 2 \textgreater\textgreater{} 1 \textgreater{} 3 -
cortical thickness: 1 \textless{} 2 = 3 - cortical\_sa: 2
\textgreater\textgreater{} 1 \textgreater{} 3 -
same\_sex\_close\_friend: 3 \textgreater\textgreater{} 2 \textgreater{}
1 - opp\_sex\_close\_friend: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
weekend\_screentime: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - weekday\_screentime: 1
\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - non\_school\_programs: 1 \textless\textless{} 2 =
3 - private\_instruction: 1 \textless{} 2 \textless{} 3 -
programs\_this\_year: 1 \textless\textless{} 2 = 3 - exercise\_time: 1
\textless{} 2 = 3 - considerate: 1 \textless{} 2 \textless{} 3 -
helps\_hurt: 3 \textgreater{} 1 = 2 - helpful: 3 \textgreater{} 1 = 2 -
family\_fight: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - family\_angry: 1 \textgreater{}
2 = 3 - family\_throw: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - family\_temper: 1
\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - family\_criticize: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
family\_hit: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - family\_peaceful: 1 \textgreater{}
2 \textgreater{} 3 - family\_outdo: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
family\_yell: 1 \textgreater{} 2 \textgreater{} 3 - parent\_perstr: 1
\textless{} 2 \textless{} 3 - parent\_anxdep: 1
\textgreater\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - parent\_withdrawn: 1 \textgreater{} 2
= 3 - parent\_somatic: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - parent\_thought: 1
\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - parent\_attention: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
parent\_aggressive: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - parent\_intrusive: 1
\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - parent\_depress: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
parent\_anxdisord: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - parent\_avoidant: 1
\textgreater{} 2 \textgreater{} 3 - parent\_antisocial: 1 \textgreater{}
2 = 3 - parent\_hyperactive: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - cbcl\_anxdep: 1
\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - cbcl\_withdep: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
cbcl\_somatic: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - cbcl\_social: 1 \textgreater{} 2
= 3 - cbcl\_thought: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - cbcl\_attention: 1
\textgreater{} 2 = 3 - cbcl\_rulebreak: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 -
cbcl\_aggressive: 1 \textgreater{} 2 = 3 - sds\_sleep: 1 \textgreater{}
2 = 3

Dennis, E., Manza, P. \& Volkow, N.D. Socioeconomic status, BMI, and
brain development in children. Transl Psychiatry 12, 33 (2022).
https://doi.org/10.1038/s41398-022-01779-3

In the study above, they looked at SES based on household income,
highest level of parental education, and area deprivation. They also
found that BMI played a role in brain development.

Orben, A., Meier, A., Dalgleish, T. et al.~Mechanisms linking social
media use to adolescent mental health vulnerability. Nat Rev Psychol
(2024). https://doi.org/10.1038/s44159-024-00307-y

\chapter{Label propagation}\label{label-propagation}

This top solution specifically came from the third imputed dataset, so
that's what we'll label propagate off of.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{all\_dls }\SpecialCharTok{|\textgreater{}} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{()}

\NormalTok{train\_data\_list\_m3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ all\_dls}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"mtbi\_train\_data\_lists"}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"imp\_3"}

\NormalTok{test\_data\_list\_m1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ all\_dls}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"mtbi\_test\_data\_lists"}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{"imp\_1"}

\NormalTok{full\_data\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge\_data\_lists}\NormalTok{(train\_data\_list\_m3, test\_data\_list\_m1)}

\CommentTok{\#full\_data\_list\_m3 \textless{}{-} list.load(input\_path("2024\_03\_17\_full\_data\_list\_m3.rds"))}
\CommentTok{\#test\_data\_list\_m3 \textless{}{-} list.load(input\_path("2024\_03\_17\_test\_data\_list\_m3.rds"))}
\CommentTok{\#test\_oom\_list\_m3 \textless{}{-} list.load(input\_path("2024\_03\_17\_test\_oom\_list\_m3.rds"))}

\NormalTok{my\_distance\_metrics }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{generate\_distance\_metrics\_list}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{continuous\_distances =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{"standard\_norm\_euclidean"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sn\_euclidean\_distance}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{discrete\_distances =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{"standard\_norm\_euclidean"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sn\_euclidean\_distance}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{ordinal\_distances =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{"standard\_norm\_euclidean"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sn\_euclidean\_distance}
\NormalTok{    ),}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{mtbi\_mc\_a }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mtbi\_rep[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, ]}

\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{mtbi\_mc\_a\_lp }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lp\_solutions\_matrix}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    mtbi\_mc\_a,}
\NormalTok{    full\_data\_list,}
